### [84] [Why Learners Drift In and Out: Examining Intermittent Discontinuance in AI-Mediated Informal Digital English Learning (AI-IDLE) Using SEM and fsQCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27493)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动框架调查了AI中介的非正式英语数字学习（AI-IDLE）中的间歇性中断行为，通过对632名中国大学EFL学习者的问卷调查，采用结构方程模型和模糊集定性比较分析发现：感知智能性、交互性和个性化通过愉悦感间接减少中断，而感知无效性、不可控性和复杂性通过无聊感间接增加中断；fsQCA进一步识别出导致间歇性中断的四种配置路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AI-IDLE研究从采纳和持续使用扩展至采纳后中断行为，填补现有研究对学习者暂时性退出AI-IDLE行为机制的理解空白。

Method: 采用问卷调查收集632名中国大学EFL学习者的数据，结合结构方程模型（SEM）和模糊集定性比较分析（fsQCA）进行数据分析。

Result: SEM结果表明：感知智能性、感知交互性和感知个性化通过愉悦感间接减少AI-IDLE间歇性中断；感知无效性、感知不可控性和感知复杂性通过无聊感间接增加中断。fsQCA结果识别出四种导致间歇性中断的配置路径，表明认知障碍和情感脱离的不同组合均可导致学习者的暂时性退出。

Conclusion: 研究扩展了AI-IDLE领域从采纳和持续使用到采纳后中断的理论边界，强调了设计愉悦、个性化、可控且认知上可管理的AI支持英语学习体验的必要性。

Abstract: This study examined intermittent discontinuance in AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE) through the cognition-affect-conation framework. Survey data were collected from 632 Chinese university EFL learners with prior AI-IDLE experience and analysed using structural equation modelling and fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis. The SEM results showed that perceived intelligence, perceived interactivity, and perceived personalisation reduced AI-IDLE intermittent discontinuance indirectly through enjoyment, whereas perceived ineffectiveness, perceived uncontrollability, and perceived complexity increased discontinuance indirectly through boredom. The fsQCA results further identified four configurational pathways leading to intermittent discontinuance, indicating that learners' temporary withdrawal from AI-IDLE can result from different combinations of cognitive barriers and affective disengagement. These findings extend AI-IDLE research from adoption and continuance to post-adoption discontinuance and highlight the need to design AI-supported English learning experiences that are enjoyable, personalised, controllable, and cognitively manageable.

</details>


### [85] [From Elastic to Viscoelastic: An EEMD-Enhanced Pulse Transit Time Model for Robust Blood Pressure Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27500)
*Boyuan Gu,Yijin Yang,Shuaiqi Cheng,Xiaorong Ding*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息驱动的无袖带血压估计框架，通过引入粘弹性补偿机制（基于EEMD分解的高频IMF定义"粘弹性速度度量"），在MIMIC-II数据库子集（364名受试者，28,525个心动周期）上实现了SBP RMSE 5.22 mmHg、DBP RMSE 3.65 mmHg、R>0.97的医学级精度，显著优于传统基于Moens-Korteweg方程的纯弹性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于脉搏波传导时间（PTT）的无袖带血压估计方法依赖Moens-Korteweg方程，该方程假设动脉壁为纯弹性体，忽略了血管固有的粘弹性特性，导致在快速血流动力学波动时估计精度显著下降。

Method: (1) 使用Modified Akima（Makima）插值对原始PPG信号进行高保真重构；(2) 采用稳健的相交切线法（Intersecting Tangent Method）精确定位脉搏波足点；(3) 利用集合经验模态分解（EEMD）分离高频本征模态函数（IMFs），定义"粘弹性速度度量"（$η\cdot \dotε$）以量化血管阻尼效应。

Result: 在MIMIC-II数据库子集（364名受试者，28,525个心动周期，高血压患病率23.4%）上验证：SBP RMSE=5.22 mmHg，DBP RMSE=3.65 mmHg，Pearson相关系数R>0.97。

Conclusion: 纳入粘弹性特征显著增强了模型对血管滞后的鲁棒性，实现了医学级无袖带血压估计精度。

Abstract: Cuffless blood pressure (BP) estimation based on Pulse Transit Time (PTT) has emerged as a promising solution for continuous health monitoring. However, conventional models relying on the Moens-Korteweg equation often fail during rapid hemodynamic fluctuations, as they assume arterial walls are purely elastic and neglect inherent viscoelasticity. To address this limitation, we propose a physics-informed framework introducing a viscoelastic compensation mechanism. First, raw photoplethysmogram (PPG) signals undergo high-fidelity reconstruction using Modified Akima (Makima) interpolation. Second, a robust Intersecting Tangent Method is applied for precise pulse foot localization. Crucially, we utilize Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD) to isolate high-frequency Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), defining a ``Viscoelastic Velocity Metric'' to quantify the vascular damping effect ($η\cdot \dotε$) typically ignored by elastic models. The framework was rigorously validated on a challenging subset of the MIMIC-II database (364 subjects, 28,525 cardiac cycles) characterized by a high prevalence of hypertension (23.4\%). Experimental results demonstrate medical-grade accuracy, yielding a Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 5.22 mmHg for Systolic and 3.65 mmHg for Diastolic BP, with Pearson correlation coefficients ($R > 0.97$). These findings confirm that incorporating viscoelastic features significantly enhances robustness against vascular hysteresis.

</details>


### [86] [Examining discontinuance of AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE) among university students: Evidence from SEM and fsQCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27506)
*Yiran Du,Huimin He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究基于认知-情感-意动框架，通过对746名中国大学生的问卷调查，发现不满意和挫折感正向预测AI辅助非正式英语学习的 discontinuance intention，其中挫折感影响更强；失验、感知复杂性和感知风险正向影响不满意和挫折感；fsQCA结果揭示了导致高 discontinuance intention 的多重充分条件组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往AI-IDLE研究多关注采用和持续使用，较少探讨用户为何在采用后放弃使用。本研究旨在填补这一空白，从后采纳阶段出发，理解学习者停止使用AI工具进行非正式英语学习的心理机制。

Method: 采用横截面问卷调查法，以746名有AI非正式英语学习经验的中国大学生为样本；使用结构方程模型（SEM）检验变量间关系，并辅以模糊集定性比较分析（fsQCA）识别导致高 discontinuance intention 的充分条件组合。

Result: SEM结果显示，不满意和挫折感均正向预测 discontinuance intention，挫折感效应更强；失验、感知复杂性和感知风险正向影响两种情感反应。fsQCA结果识别出多条导致高 discontinuance intention 的因果路径，表明该现象由因果复杂性和等效性决定。

Conclusion: 研究将AI-IDLE研究从采用和参与拓展至后采纳阶段的放弃行为，为降低学习者在使用AI进行非正式英语学习时的不满意、挫折感、感知复杂性和感知风险提供了实践启示。

Abstract: This study examined university students' discontinuance intention towards AI-mediated informal digital learning of English (AI-IDLE). Drawing on the cognition-affect-conation framework, the study investigated how three cognitive factors, namely disconfirmation, perceived complexity, and perceived risk, influence two affective responses, namely dissatisfaction and frustration, and how these affective responses predict discontinuance intention. A cross-sectional survey was conducted with 746 Chinese university students who had experience using AI tools for informal English learning. Data were analysed using structural equation modelling (SEM) and fuzzy-set qualitative comparative analysis (fsQCA). The SEM results showed that dissatisfaction and frustration positively predicted discontinuance intention, with frustration showing the stronger effect. Disconfirmation, perceived complexity, and perceived risk also positively influenced dissatisfaction and frustration. The fsQCA results further identified multiple sufficient configurations leading to high AI-IDLE discontinuance intention, indicating that discontinuance is shaped by causal complexity and equifinality rather than by a single necessary condition. These findings extend AI-IDLE research from adoption and engagement to post-adoption disengagement and provide implications for reducing learners' dissatisfaction, frustration, perceived complexity, and risk in AI-supported informal English learning.

</details>


### [87] [I'm Fine, But My Voice Isn't: Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection for Reflective Journaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27517)
*Sumin Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了跨模态情感 dissonance 检测（CADD）任务，用于识别数字日记中文本与语音之间的情感不一致（掩蔽、应对、一致），构建了 CADD-Journal 数据集、DACM 双编码器模型，并揭示了 TTS 训练与真实语音之间的领域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字日记存在"真实性缺口"：用户即使私下写作也会不自觉地将原始情感转化为更规范的文本，导致文本与语音情感不一致。现有情感分析忽略这种跨模态 dissonance，需要一种定向三路分类来检测掩蔽、应对和一致三种状态。

Method: (1) 构建 CADD-Journal：1,800样本的 TTS 数据集，采用共享句子池设计隔离声学信号与文本内容；(2) 提出 DACM 双编码器模型，引入非对称跨模态注意力解决池化融合中的梯度退化问题；(3) 在三个自然语料库上进行零样本评估量化领域差距；(4) 开发 ReflectJournal iOS 原型应用。

Result: DACM 模型达到 macro-F1 0.711；四步消融显示非对称注意力是主要驱动因素（+0.242），而 DIM 仅在跨模态特征上有效（+0.033）；零样本评估揭示 TTS 训练模型与真实语音之间存在显著领域差距。

Conclusion: CADD 任务和 DACM 模型有效检测文本-语音情感 dissonance；非对称注意力机制是性能提升的关键；现有 TTS 训练模型尚无法直接迁移到真实语音，需要未来自然语料库满足两个具体要求。

Abstract: Digital journaling creates an authenticity gap: users consciously translate raw emotions into text, often sanitizing narratives even in private writing. We formalize this as Cross-Modal Affective Dissonance Detection (CADD), a directional three-way classification distinguishing Masking (positive text, negative acoustics), Coping (negative text, positive acoustics), and Congruent utterances, grounded in Gross's process model of emotion regulation. We present three further contributions: (i) CADD-Journal, a 1,800-sample TTS dataset with a shared-sentence-pool design that provably isolates acoustic signal from textual content; (ii) DACM, a dual-encoder model with asymmetric cross-modal attention that re-solves a gradient degeneracy in pooled fusion, achieving macro-F1 0.711 - with a four-step ablation demonstrating that asymmetric attention is the dominant driver (+ 0.242) while the DIM is effective only on cross-modal features (+0.033); and (iii) a domain gap quantification: zero-shot evaluation across three naturalistic corpora reveals a substantial gap between TTS-trained models and real speech, and we identify two concrete requirements for future in-the-wild corpus construction. ReflectJournal, a proof-of-concept iOS application, operationalizes the framework and provides a deployment platform for naturalistic data collection.

</details>


### [88] [lpviz: Interactive Linear Programming Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27518)
*Evan Grand,Michael Klamkin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍 lpviz，一个用于线性规划的可视化工具，支持交互式编辑可行域和目标向量，可比较多种 LP 算法（Simplex、Interior-Point、Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient、Central Path）的行为，并在 3D 模式下展示迭代元数据（如互补间隙、KKT 残差），已用于研究和教学，开源免费可访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性规划可视化工具多依赖原始数值系数的手动操作，直观性不足，且缺乏对不同算法行为的横向比较支持，难以在教学和研究中帮助用户建立算法行为直觉。

Method: 开发了一个基于浏览器的深度交互式可视化工具 lpviz，支持用户直接绘制和编辑可行域及目标向量，集成了 Simplex、Interior-Point、Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient、Central Path 四类算法，并在 3D 模式下将迭代点的垂直高度映射为互补间隙或 KKT 残差等元数据。

Result: lpviz 在研究和课堂教学中得到了实际应用，帮助用户直观理解不同求解器的优劣势以及求解器设置对收敛行为的影响。

Conclusion: lpviz 是一个开源、许可宽松、跨设备的线性规划可视化工具，能够有效辅助算法教学与研究方向上的直觉培养。

Abstract: This paper presents lpviz, a browser-based visualization tool for linear programming. lpviz is deeply interactive, offering an intuitive interface where users can directly draw and edit the feasible region and objective vector, without requiring cumbersome manipulation of raw numerical coefficients. lpviz lets users compare the behavior of several classes of linear programming algorithms, namely Simplex, Interior-Point, Primal-Dual Hybrid Gradient, and Central Path. In the 3D mode, lpviz places iterates at heights corresponding to important solver metadata such as complementarity gap or KKT residual, helping users gain further insight into algorithm behavior beyond the primal iterates alone. lpviz has been used in both research and classroom settings, to help develop intuition for the strengths and weaknesses of different solvers and the impact of solver settings on convergence behavior. lpviz is open-source, permissively licensed, and freely available on any device with a web browser at https://lpviz.net .

</details>


### [89] [Knowledge Affordances for Hybrid Human-AI Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27539)
*Irene Celino*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了"知识可供性"（Knowledge Affordance, KA）概念，用于系统化描述在混合人机环境中，智能体如何识别有意义的寻求信息机会。KA是声明性的、基于语义的，描述知识源能提供什么、回答何种问题以及具有哪些上下文属性，并强调其关系性——取决于任务、偏好和情境因素。这是一项连接可供性、语义Web服务、知识工程等多个研究领域的概念性提案，并勾勒了构建KA感知系统的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着信息生态系统的异质性增强，人类和人工智能体面临一个基本但未解决的问题：寻求知识时应该问谁、为什么。作者受人类"阅读房间"的直觉启发，希望系统化描述智能体如何识别信息寻求的有意义机会。

Method: 概念性提案：提出知识可供性（KA）作为声明性、基于语义的描述，刻画知识源能提供什么、适合哪些问题类型以及具有何种上下文属性；强调KA的关系性（源于任务、偏好与情境因素的相互作用）；连接可供性理论、语义Web服务、知识工程和查询、互理解性等多个研究流。

Result: 提出了KA概念框架，但本文尚未进行实验验证，主要贡献在于概念构建和未来研究方向勾勒。

Conclusion: KA概念为构建KA感知系统提供了理论基础，有望使信息空间的导航更加透明、适应性强且具有共享理解，为混合人机环境中的知识寻求问题提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: As information ecosystems grow more heterogeneous, both humans and artificial agents increasingly face a simple yet unresolved question: when seeking knowledge, whom should we ask, and why? Inspired by how people intuitively "read a room", this paper introduces the concept of knowledge affordance (KA) to systematize how agents identify meaningful opportunities for information seeking in hybrid human-AI environments.
  Rather than introducing a fully formed framework, we propose KAs as declarative, semantically grounded descriptions of what a knowledge source can offer, for which kinds of questions, and with which contextual properties. Additionally, we suggest that KAs are relational, possibly emerging from the interplay between the agent's task, preferences and situational factors.
  Our contribution is thus a conceptual proposal that connects different research streams, including affordances, semantic web services, knowledge engineering and querying, and mutual intelligibility. We sketch possible research directions to build KA-aware systems that navigate information spaces with greater transparency, adaptability and shared understanding.

</details>


### [90] [Users' Activity Logs: the Good, the Bad, the Misconception, and the Disastrous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27676)
*Eman Alashwali*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对30名沙特阿拉伯Google个人账户持有者的访谈数据进行二次分析，从积极、消极、灾难性以及误解四个维度，全面探讨了用户对活动日志的感知，并提出了实用建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多聚焦于用户活动日志的负面方面（如隐私担忧和缺乏意识），缺乏对用户感知的全面平衡理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，同时考察积极、消极、灾难性方面及用户误解。

Method: 对30名沙特阿拉伯Google个人账户持有者的访谈数据进行二次分析，采用模板分析法（Template Analysis），从"好的、坏的、误解的、灾难性的"四个主题视角进行编码分析。

Result: 揭示了新的主题和使用案例，提供了用户对活动日志感知的平衡视图，涵盖积极效用、隐私担忧、误解认知以及潜在灾难性后果。

Conclusion: 提出了面向服务提供商、隐私研究人员/专家以及用户的实用建议，为后续相关研究提供了更深入的理解和有价值的参考来源。

Abstract: Most service providers, such as Google, save logs from data generated by users while using the service. Many service providers provide users with privacy controls to manage whether, how, and for how long the data is saved and used by the service provider. While most prior studies focused on the negative side of users' activity logs, such as users' lack of awareness about the logs' privacy controls and users' privacy concerns toward their data, this work aims to provide a balanced view of users' perceptions regarding activity logs by considering the positive, negative, and extremely negative (hence disastrous) sides, as well as the misconceptions of activity logs. In this work, we present a case study of Google's Activity controls by conducting a secondary analysis of interview data from 30 Google personal account holders in Saudi Arabia. Using template analysis, we analyzed the data from the lens of four main themes: the good, the bad, the misconception, and the disastrous aspects of users' activity logs from the users' perspective. Our findings uncover new themes and use cases, offering a balanced view of users' perceptions of activity logs, and provide a better understanding and a useful source for subsequent studies on related topics. We conclude with practical recommendations for service providers, privacy researchers and experts, and users alike.

</details>


### [91] [AgentEconomist: An End-to-end Agentic System Translating Economic Intuitions into Executable Computational Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27725)
*Jiaju Chen,Jinghua Piao,Xia Xu,Songwei Li,Tong Xia,Xiangnan He,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AgentEconomist是一个端到端交互式系统，通过模块化多阶段架构（想法开发、实验设计、实验执行），将经济学直觉转化为可执行的计算实验，基于超过13,000篇高质量论文的知识库，其生成的研究想法在文献基础、新颖性和洞察力方面优于通用大语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济学研究面临的核心挑战不在于缺乏直觉，而在于将直觉洞察转化为可验证研究的困难。现有方法缺乏将抽象直觉系统性地转化为可执行计算实验的端到端工具。

Method: 构建了覆盖13,000+高质量论文的领域知识库，采用模块化多阶段架构：(1) Idea Development Stage——生成基于文献的假设；(2) Experimental Design Stage——配置与模拟器对齐的实验参数和协议；(3) Experimental Execution Stage——运行实验并返回结构化分析。整体形成人机交互、迭代式的工作流程。

Result: 通过人类专家评估和LLM作为评判员的实验表明，该系统生成的研究想法在文献基础、新颖性和洞察力方面均优于最先进的通用大语言模型。

Conclusion: AgentEconomist采用人机协作范式，使研究者能够专注于高层直觉，而将繁琐的翻译和计算执行过程委托给智能体，从而有效弥合经济学直觉与可验证研究之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: A long-standing challenge in economics lies not in the lack of intuition, but in the difficulty of translating intuitive insights into verifiable research. To address this challenge, we introduce AgentEconomist, an end-to-end interactive system designed to translate abstract intuitions into executable computational experiments. Grounded in a domain-specific knowledge base covering over 13,000 high-quality academic papers, the system employs a modular multi-stage architecture. Specifically, the Idea Development Stage generates literature-grounded hypotheses, the Experimental Design Stage configures simulator-aligned experimental parameters and protocols, and the Experimental Execution Stage runs experiments and returns structured analyses. Together, these stages form a human-in-the-loop, iterative workflow that translates economic intuitions into executable computational experiments. Through extensive experiments involving human expert evaluation and large language models (LLMs) as judges, we show that the system generates research ideas with stronger literature grounding and higher novelty and insight than state-of-the-art generic LLMs. Overall, AgentEconomist adopts a human-AI collaboration paradigm that enables researchers to focus on high-level intuitions, while delegating the labor-intensive processes of translation and computational execution to agents.

</details>


### [92] ["It depends on where AI is used": Players' attitude patterns and evaluative logics toward different AI applications in digital games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27812)
*Ting-Chen Hsu,Jiangxu Lin,Wenran Chen,Fei Qin,Zheyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于310份问卷的1,856条开放回答，研究玩家对游戏AI在八种应用场景（智能NPC、涌现叙事、动态平衡、推荐系统、审查治理、美术资产生成、协同创作、玩法演化）中的接受模式，识别出接受、拒绝、条件接受的三种态度及其背后的六种评价逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在数字游戏中日益普及，但玩家态度不仅取决于是否使用AI，还取决于AI在何处以及如何介入游戏过程，现有研究缺乏对多场景下玩家评价模式的系统性理解。

Method: 收集310份问卷中的1,856条有效开放式回答，采用主题分析法识别玩家对八种AI应用场景的接受、拒绝和条件接受的原因。

Result: 玩家在AI增强沉浸感、个性化、新颖性、效率或便捷性时持欢迎态度；但当AI威胁创造力、情感真实性、自主性、公平性、系统稳定性、作者身份或问责性时则持抵触态度。进一步归纳出六种评价逻辑：体验丰富化、工具性效率、系统可靠性、能动性与控制、作者身份与合规、人工监督。

Conclusion: 数字游戏中AI接受具有高度情境敏感性，玩家的评价逻辑多元且对立，初步发现为理解AI在游戏中的接受机制提供了框架性基础。

Abstract: As AI becomes increasingly embedded in digital games, players' attitudes de-pend not only on whether AI is used, but also on where and how it intervenes in gameplay. This study examines players' evaluative patterns toward eight AI application contexts, including intelligent NPCs, emergent narrative, dynamic balancing, recommendation systems, review and governance, art asset generation, co-creation gameplay, and gameplay evolution. Based on 1,856 valid open-ended responses from 310 questionnaires, we conducted thematic analysis to identify reasons for acceptance, rejection, and conditional acceptance. Results show that players welcomed AI when it enhanced immersion, personalization, novelty, efficiency, or convenience, but resisted it when it threatened creativity, emotional authenticity, autonomy, fairness, system stability, authorship, or accountability. We further identify six evaluative logics: experiential enrichment, instrumental efficiency, system reliability, agency and control, authorship and compliance, and human oversight. These preliminary findings highlight the context-sensitive nature of AI acceptance in digital games.

</details>


### [93] [CoNewsReader: Supporting Comprehensive Understanding and Raising Critical Thoughts on Social Media News Through Comments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27905)
*Kangyu Yuan,Guanzheng Chen,Sizhe Liang,Hehai Lin,Qingyu Guo,Dingdong Liu,Xiaojuan Ma,Zhenhui Peng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoNewsReader，一种基于评论的互动式新闻批判性阅读工具，利用大语言模型帮助用户从评论中获取补充信息、筛选有用评论并生成批判性问题。用户研究（N=24）表明，与基线社交媒体新闻界面相比，CoNewsReader能提升用户的新闻理解、批判性思考能力和阅读参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批判性新闻阅读（CNR）对普通人来说具有挑战性，而新闻评论可提供互补信息和多元批判性观点以辅助CNR，但如何利用评论支持CNR尚缺乏研究。

Method: 通过文献和形成性研究（N=12）推导用户需求，开发基于大语言模型的CoNewsReader工具，并进行受试者内实验（N=24）与基线社交媒体界面对比评估。

Result: 使用CoNewsReader的用户在新闻理解和批判性思考方面表现更好，阅读体验更深入，且能更有效地利用评论进行批判性阅读。

Conclusion: 基于评论的CNR工具能有效支持新闻批判性阅读，未来应考虑如何结合用户生成内容与机器生成内容来辅助阅读理解任务。

Abstract: Critical news reading (CNR), which requires grasping the holistic ideas of and raising critical thoughts on the news, is beneficial yet challenging for general people who usually get information on daily social media. Comments under the news can aid CNR by providing complementary information and other readers' diverse and critical thoughts. However, it is under-investigated how to leverage these comments to support users in CNR. In this paper, we first derive user requirements for a comment-based CNR tool from literature and a formative study (N=12). Then, we develop CoNewsReader, a comment-based interactive CNR tool powered by a large language model. CoNewsReader supports users in grasping the news idea with complementary information from comments, filtering useful comments for CNR, and getting questions generated based on the comments to conduct critical thinking. Our within-subjects study with 24 university students indicates that compared to a baseline news reading interface in social media, participants with CoNewsReader have a more engaging CNR experience and perform better on comprehending the news and raising critical thoughts. We discuss design considerations for supporting reading tasks with user- and machine-generated content.

</details>


### [94] [Enhancing multimodal affect recognition in healthcare: the robustness of appraisal dimensions over labels within age groups and in cross-age generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27938)
*Hippolyte Fournier,Sina Alisamir,Safaa Azzakhnini,Isabella Zsoldos,Eléonore Trân,Gérard Bailly,Frédéric Elisei,Béatrice Bouchot,Brice Varini,Patrick Constant,Joan Fruitet,Franck Tarpin-Bernard,Solange Rossato,François Portet,Olivier Koenig,Hanna Chainay,Fabien Ringeval*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于评价理论维度的多模态情感识别模型相比基于类别标签的模型在跨年龄组（老年人和年轻人）情感识别中的表现，发现评价维度在预测准确性和跨年龄泛化能力上显著优于类别标签，后者在跨语料库评估中表现降至随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感识别在AI辅助医疗（如计算机化认知训练）中仍面临挑战，尤其是面向老年人群体的多模态情感识别。现有研究缺乏对不同年龄组间情感识别模型泛化能力的系统比较，以及评价理论维度与类别标签两种范式在跨年龄场景下的优劣评估。

Method: 基于THERADIA-WoZ语料库（老年人）和新增的年轻人数据集，构建多模态情感识别模型；采用评价理论维度（appraisal dimensions）与类别标签（categorical labels）两种范式；通过语料库内（within-corpus）、跨语料库（cross-corpus）和混合语料库（mixed-corpus）三种评估设置进行对比实验。

Result: 评价维度在所有评估条件下均优于类别标签，预测准确性和稳定性更高；类别标签在跨语料库评估中性能降至随机水平，无法跨年龄泛化；评价维度在跨年龄场景中仍保持高于随机水平的预测性能；混合训练未能提升跨年龄泛化能力。

Conclusion: 评价理论维度在情感计算中具有理论和实践上的优势，优于类别标签范式；多模态融合和深度学习表征对情感建模至关重要；研究为行为科学提供了时间连续情感预测的API工具。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI) into healthcare has advanced significantly, yet affect recognition remains a major challenge, particularly in AI-assisted interventions such as Computerized Cognitive Training (CCT). The THERADIA-WoZ corpus was developed to enable multimodal affect recognition in the context of AI-driven CCT, focusing on an older adult population. This study extends the corpus by introducing a dataset collected from young adults, allowing direct comparison of affect recognition models across age groups. Our objective was to assess whether multimodal models based on dimensions borrowed from appraisal theories outperform those based on categorical labels and to evaluate their generalisation power across age corpora. After comparing both corpora, models were trained and tested using within-corpus, cross-corpus, and mixed-corpus evaluation. Results revealed that appraisal dimensions consistently outperformed categorical labels across all conditions, demonstrating greater predictive accuracy and stability. Notably, categorical labels failed to generalise across age corpora, as performance dropped to chance levels in cross-corpus evaluation. In contrast, appraisal dimensions maintained predictive performance above chance, reinforcing their robustness for cross-age affect recognition. Furthermore, training on both corpora did not improve generalisation beyond within-corpus training. The findings support the theoretical and practical advantages of appraisal dimensions over categorical labels in affective computing. They also highlight the importance of multimodal fusion and deep learning representations for emotion modeling. To facilitate future research, we provide an API for researchers interested in time-continuous emotion prediction, offering valuable tools for behavioral sciences to enhance the measurement of emotional states in various experimental settings.

</details>


### [95] [MyoKin3X: A Myoelectric Framework for Full-Hand 3D Force Recording](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27949)
*Charlotte Rohleder,Raul Sîmpetru,Annika Wünsch,Alessandro Del Vecchio*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: MyoKin3X是一个可定制的全手框架，能够同时测量五个手指的三维力，集成了3D力传感器、同步肌电采集、交叉校准、坐标变换及多种反馈模式，校准稳定性高（变异系数0.04%）、串扰低（减少92.71%）、预测精度高（R² > 0.99）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在手指覆盖范围、力维度与解剖适应性之间存在权衡，难以实现可靠的全手同步多方向力测量，缺乏多轴校准、手尺寸适配及手指特异性力重建的标准化方案。

Method: 提出MyoKin3X框架，包含五个集成3D力传感器、交叉校准算法、自动坐标变换、同步EMG采集软件，以及四种反馈模式（1D斜坡、疲劳协议、2D任意目标斜坡、2D探索任务）。

Result: 校准因子变异系数均值0.04%，50N时最大力误差±0.06N；轴间串扰减少92.71%，残差串扰低于0.02%；所有传感器R² > 0.99。

Conclusion: MyoKin3X为标准化全手力采集提供了验证可靠的测量精度、灵活的任务控制与实时可视化，适用于手部运动控制、肌肉协同与人机接口研究。

Abstract: Simultaneous multi-directional force measurement across all five digits is essential for studying hand coordination, compensatory forces, and myoelectric control, yet existing systems trade off digit coverage, force dimensionality, and anatomical adaptability. Reliable full-hand acquisition remains challenging because multi-axis calibration, hand-size adjustment, and consistent digit-specific force reconstruction are technically demanding. We present MyoKin3X, a customizable full-hand framework for simultaneous 3D force measurement of up to five digits providing robust and validated force reconstruction. It combines an anatomically versatile structure with five integrated 3D force sensors and a standalone software for synchronized electromyography and force acquisition. MyoKin3X provides in-place cross-calibration of all five sensors, single- and multi-digit maximal voluntary contraction recording, and automated coordinate transformation to digit-specific coordinate systems for standardized analysis across subjects and tasks. Calibration validation demonstrates high stability of the axis-specific calibration factors, with a mean coefficient of variation of 0.04% and maximum force error of +- 0.06N at 50N. It also shows effective inter-axis decoupling (mean crosstalk reduction: 92.71%; residual crosstalk below 0.02% for most axis pairs) and high predictive accuracy (R2 > 0.99 across sensors). The software includes four feedback modes: 1D ramps, fatigue protocols, 2D arbitrary target ramps, and 2D exploratory tasks. MyoKin3X therefore enables standardized full-hand force acquisition with validated measurement reliability, flexible protocol control, and real-time visualization for high-fidelity studies of hand motor control, muscle synergies, and human-machine interfacing.

</details>


### [96] [When and How AI Should Assist Brainstorming for AI Impact Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27997)
*Jarod Govers,Sanja Šćepanović,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了AI如何支持团队在AI影响评估中进行头脑风暴。通过5轮（28人）协同设计工作坊和10轮（54人）评估工作坊，发现AI能提升通用型AI用例的影响评估质量和头脑风暴感知，但对专业型AI用例效果不显著。最终提出了AI辅助头脑风暴的设计指南：AI应在早期构思阶段仅提供提示而非解决方案，在收敛阶段帮助结构化想法，利用专业知识完善想法，总体应支持繁琐过程而非团队珍视的创意构思本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI影响评估工具未针对协作团队头脑风暴场景设计，也无法捕捉多元团队中的不同观点，缺乏支持团队头脑风暴的AI工具。

Method: 首先从战略预见中改编两种结构化方法，在5场（28人）工作坊中协同设计AI干预措施；随后在10场（54人）工作坊中评估干预效果。

Result: AI提升了通用AI用例（聊天机器人伴侣）的影响评估质量和头脑风暴感知，但对专业AI用例（肾脏分配应用）效果不显著。

Conclusion: AI在头脑风暴中应：早期构思仅提供提示而非解决方案；在参与者陷入定势或饱和时主动介入；在收敛阶段帮助结构化想法；利用专业知识完善想法；总体应支持繁琐过程而非团队珍视的创意构思。

Abstract: A key task in AI practice is to assess potential impacts to prevent harm. Current AI tools assisting AI impact assessment have not been designed or evaluated for collaborative team brainstorming, and they do not capture the range of views in diverse teams. We studied how AI can support team brainstorming during AI impact assessment and made three contributions. First, we adapted two structured methods from strategic foresight and co-designed AI interventions for them in five in-person workshops with 28 participants in total. Second, we evaluated the interventions in ten in-person workshops with 54 participants, finding that AI improved impact assessment quality and brainstorming perceptions for a general-purpose AI use (a chatbot companion) but not for a specialised one (a kidney allocation application). Third, our findings result in broader design guidance for AI assistance in brainstorming: AI should only offer hints and not solutions during early ideation, initiating interaction only when participants face fixation or saturation; it should facilitate structuring ideas during convergence; leverage expertise to refine ideas; and overall, it should serve more in support of tedious brainstorming process tasks, rather than ideation that teams value to do themselves.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [ANCORA: Learning to Question via Manifold-Anchored Self-Play for Verifiable Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27644)
*Chengcao Yang,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ANCORA框架，通过让语言模型交替扮演问题生成者（Proposer）和问题解决者（Solver），利用可验证问题的反馈实现无需人工监督的自我改进。在Dafny2Verus基准上，pass@1从SFT基线的26.6%提升至81.5%，超越PSV自对弈基线15.8个百分点；并在迁移场景下在MBPP和HumanEval上分别达到36.2%和17.2% pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型能否摆脱对人工标注数据的依赖，通过自我生成可验证问题、求解问题并利用反馈进行自我改进，实现从"学习回答"到"学习提问"的范式转变。

Method: 提出锚定课程框架ANCORA，包含：(1) 统一策略交替执行Proposer（生成规格说明）和Solver（生成验证解）角色；(2) 两级组相对更新机制，将Proposer优势跨规格耦合与Solver优势跨求解尝试耦合；(3) 迭代自蒸馏SFT，在RL前将基座模型投影到有效输出流形上；(4) UCB引导的课程DAG，仅通过严格筛选的新颖且被Solver验证的规格扩展。

Result: 在Verus实例化中，Dafny2Verus pass@1从SFT基线26.6%提升至81.5%（测试时训练+零样本评估），超越PSV自对弈基线（使用1-shot推理）15.8个百分点；迁移设置下，从Dafny2Verus种子训练后在MBPP和HumanEval上分别取得36.2%和17.2% pass@1。

Conclusion: 稀疏验证器反馈即使在使用MLRL对齐奖励时也会导致Proposer崩溃，而ANCORA的三个稳定机制（两级组相对更新、自蒸馏SFT、UCB课程DAG）是必要的；该方法展示了无需人工监督即可通过自我提问-求解循环持续改进语言模型能力的可行性。

Abstract: We propose a paradigm shift from learning to answer to learning to question: can a language model generate verifiable problems, solve them, and turn the resulting feedback into self-improvement without human supervision? We introduce ANCORA, an anchored-curriculum framework in which a unified policy alternates between a Proposer that synthesizes novel specifications and a Solver that produces verified solutions. ANCORA rests on three load-bearing mechanisms: a two-level group-relative update that couples Proposer advantages across specifications with Solver advantages across solution attempts; iterative self-distilled SFT that projects the base model onto its valid-output manifold before RL; and a UCB-guided Curriculum DAG that grows only through strictly filtered, novel, Solver-verified specifications. These stabilizers are necessary because sparse verifier feedback otherwise drives Proposer collapse even under MLRL-aligned rewards. Instantiated in Verus, ANCORA lifts Dafny2Verus pass@1 from a 26.6% SFT baseline to 81.5% in the test-time-training setting under 0-shot evaluation, outperforming the PSV self-play baseline by 15.8 points despite PSV using 1-shot inference; in a separate transfer setting, training from Dafny2Verus seeds yields 36.2% and 17.2% pass@1 on held-out MBPP and HumanEval.

</details>


### [98] [Simple Self-Conditioning Adaptation for Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26985)
*Michael Cardei,Huu Binh Ta,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自条件掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），一种无需重新训练即可改进掩码扩散模型的后训练适配方法。该方法通过让每个去噪步骤基于模型之前的干净状态预测进行条件化，在不增加额外推理开销的情况下，显著提升了生成质量，在多个领域（文本、图像、分子、基因组）均取得了稳定改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准掩码扩散模型中，若某位置在反向更新后仍保持掩码状态，模型会丢弃该位置之前的干净状态预测，导致仍需从掩码标记重新推断，限制了跨步骤的精细化改进能力。

Method: 提出自条件掩码扩散模型（SCMDM），作为后训练适配方法，每个去噪步骤基于模型自身上一轮的干净状态预测进行条件化。仅需最小架构修改，不引入循环隐状态通路，不依赖辅助参考模型，不增加额外去噪器评估次数。

Result: 在OWT训练模型上生成困惑度从42.89降至23.72（降低近50%），在离散图像合成、小分子生成和基因组分布建模方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 部分自条件化（包括常用的50% dropout策略）在后训练场景中并非最优；当模型自生成的干净状态估计变得有信息量时，专门进行精细化优于混合条件和无条件目标。SCMDM提供了一种简单有效的后训练改进方案。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate discrete sequences by iterative denoising under an absorbing masking process. In standard masked diffusion, if a token remains masked after a reverse update, the model discards its clean-state prediction for that position. Thus, still-masked positions must be repeatedly inferred from the mask token alone. This design choice limits cross-step refinement. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a simple, yet effective, post-training adaptation for MDMs that conditions each denoising step on the model's own previous clean-state predictions. The resulting method, called Self-Conditioned Masked Diffusion Models (SCMDM), requires minimal architectural change, does not introduce a recurrent latent-state pathway, does not rely on an auxiliary reference model, and adds no extra denoiser evaluations during sampling. This is an important departure from partial self-conditioning approaches which requires expensive model training from scratch. In particular, the paper shows that partial self-conditioning, including the commonly used 50% dropout strategy for training self-conditioned models from scratch, is suboptimal in the post-training regime. Instead, once the model's self-generated clean-state estimates become informative, the specialization to refinement is preferable to mixing conditional and unconditional objectives. SCMDM is evaluated across multiple domains, demonstrating consistent improvement over vanilla MDM baselines, achieving nearly a 50% reduction in generative perplexity on OWT-trained models (42.89 to 23.72), alongside strong improvements in discretized image synthesis quality, small molecular generation, and enhanced fidelity in genomic distribution modeling.

</details>


### [99] [People-Centred Medical Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26991)
*Zheng Zhang,Milad Masroor,Cuong Nguyen,Tahir Hassan,Yuanhong Chen,David Rosewarne,Kevin Wells,Thanh-Toan Do,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PecMan框架，通过动态门控机制联合优化医疗AI的公平性、诊断准确性和工作流效率，以解决临床采纳率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据中心的医疗AI虽诊断准确率高，但因忽视了跨人群公平性优化和工作流整合，导致临床采纳率低；先前的工作流整合（如L2D、L2C）和AI公平性研究相互孤立，未考虑临床环境中的实际约束（如医生可用性有限）。

Method: 提出People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan)，一种人-AI协作框架，通过动态门控机制在医生工作量约束下将病例分配给AI、医生或两者共同处理，联合优化公平性、诊断准确性和工作流有效性。

Result: 引入Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI)基准评估准确率、公平性和医生工作量之间的权衡，实验表明PecMan一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: PecMan通过联合优化公平性与工作流整合，为更可信赖和临床可行的AI系统铺平道路。

Abstract: Recent advances in data-centric medical AI have produced highly accurate diagnostic systems, but the emphasis on data curation and performance metrics has not translated into widespread clinical adoption. We conjecture that this limited uptake stems from insufficient attention dedicated to the optimisation of fair performance across diverse patient populations and to workflow integration: performance biases can create regulatory barriers, and poorly integrated automation can disrupt clinical routines, degrade the quality of human-AI collaboration, and reduce clinicians' willingness to adopt AI tools. Prior work on workflow integration (e.g., Learning to Defer (L2D) and Learning to Complement (L2C)) and AI fairness has typically examined these challenges in isolation, overlooking their natural interdependence and the practical constraints of clinical environments, such as restricted clinician availability. We propose People-Centred Medical Image Analysis (PecMan), a human-AI framework that jointly optimises fairness, diagnostic accuracy, and workflow effectiveness through a dynamic gating mechanism that assigns cases to AI, clinicians, or both under clinician workload constraints. We also introduce the Fairness and Human-Centred AI (FairHAI) benchmark for evaluating trade-offs between accuracy, fairness, and clinician workload. Experiments using this benchmark show that PecMan consistently outperforms existing methods, paving the way for more trustworthy and clinically viable AI systems. Code will be available upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [100] [Automatic Causal Fairness Analysis with LLM-Generated Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27011)
*Alessia Berarducci,Eric Rossetto,Alessandro Antonucci,Marco Zaffalon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairMind原型系统，利用标准公平模型和反事实因果效应分析，在数据集层面自动评估机器学习中的公平性问题，并通过LLM生成零样本公平性报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数AutoML框架没有考虑训练数据和预测中潜在的公平性问题，需要一种自动化的公平性分析方法。

Method: 基于Plečko和Bareinboim提出的标准公平模型，通过反事实查询计算因果效应（考虑目标、混淆变量、中介变量和保护特征的不同取值），实现封闭形式计算，并利用LLM以零样本方式生成公平性评估报告。

Result: 通过示例展示了该方法相比LLM直接分析的优势，并扩展到有序分类保护变量、连续目标变量以及新的分解结果。

Conclusion: FairMind能够自动化数据层面的公平性分析，为AutoML系统引入公平性评估能力，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: AutoML, intended as the process of automating the application of machine learning to real-world problems, is a key step for AI popularisation. Most AutoML frameworks are not accounting for the potential lack of fairness in the training data and in the corresponding predictions. We introduce \textsc{FairMind}, a software prototype aiming to automatise fairness analysis at the dataset level. We achieve that by resorting to the assumptions of the \emph{standard fairness model}, recently proposed by Plečko and Bareinboim. This allows for a sound fairness evaluation in terms of causal effects, based on \emph{counterfactual} queries involving the target, possibly confounders and mediators, and the different values of an input feature we regard as \emph{protected}. After the necessary data preprocessing, the tool implements a closed-form computation of the effects. LLMs are consequently exploited to generate accurate reports on the fairness levels detected in the training dataset. We achieve that in a zero-shot setup and show by examples the expected advantages with respect to a direct analysis performed by the LLM. To favour applications, extensions to ordinal protected variable and continuous targets and novel decomposition results are also discussed.

</details>


### [101] [Fidelity, Diversity, and Privacy: A Multi-Dimensional LLM Evaluation for Clinical Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27014)
*Guillermo Iglesias,Gema Bello-Orgaz,María Navas-Loro,Cristian Ramirez-Atencia,Mercè Salvador Robert,Enrique Baca-Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5等LLM生成基于ICD-10编码的精神健康评估报告，并通过语义保真度、词汇多样性和隐私/抄袭三个维度的评估框架，证明生成的合成数据在临床连贯性、多样性和隐私安全性方面均表现良好，可有效扩充临床NLP训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量标注医学数据（尤其是精神健康领域）稀缺，隐私法规限制数据共享，导致鲁棒机器学习模型训练存在瓶颈，亟需替代性数据生成方案。

Method: 使用DeepSeek-R1、OpenBioLLM-Llama3和Qwen 3.5三种LLM，基于特定ICD-10编码条件生成合成精神健康评估报告；引入包含语义保真度、词汇多样性和隐私/抄袭三个维度的综合评估框架对生成文本进行质量评估。

Result: 所有模型均能生成临床连贯、多样且隐私安全的合成报告，有效扩充了临床NLP可用训练数据，且未损害患者保密性。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的合成数据生成是缓解精神健康领域数据稀缺问题的可行方案，所提出的三维评估框架能有效保障生成数据的质量与安全性。

Abstract: The scarcity of high-quality annotated medical data, particularly in mental health, poses a significant bottleneck for training robust machine learning models. Privacy regulations restrict data sharing, making synthetic data generation a promising alternative. The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) in a data augmentation pipeline could be leveraged as an alternative in this field. In the proposed methodology, DeepSeek-R1, OpenBioLLM-Llama3 and Qwen 3.5 are used to generate synthetic mental health evaluation reports conditioned on specific International Classification of Diseases, Tenth Revision (ICD-10) codes. Because naive text generation can lead to mode collapse or privacy breaches (memorization), a comprehensive evaluation framework is introduced. The generated diagnostic texts are assessed across three dimensions: semantic fidelity, lexical diversity, and privacy/plagiarism. The results demonstrate that all models can generate clinically coherent, diverse, and privacy-safe synthetic reports, significantly expanding the available training data for clinical natural language processing tasks without compromising patient confidentiality.

</details>


### [102] [Dynamic Adversarial Fine-Tuning Reorganizes Refusal Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27019)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen,Yijun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过测量驱动的方法，系统揭示了动态对抗微调（R2D2）如何改变安全对齐语言模型中的拒绝载体（refusal carrier），发现模型并非简单地发生拒绝方向漂移，而是经历了拒绝载体的重新组织（从后期层迁移到早期层），且这种改变伴随着安全性与实用性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究虽然刻画了拒绝方向和对齐鲁棒性，但未能解释动态对抗微调如何在训练过程中改变拒绝载体（refusal carrier），即模型内部负责拒绝有害请求的表示结构。

Method: 使用一个7B骨干模型，在监督微调（SFT）和R2D2风格的动态对抗微调下，结合固定来源的HarmBench、StrongREJECT和XSTest基准，以及五锚点拒绝几何套件和因果干预方法进行系统测量。

Result: R2D2在第50和100步时将HarmBench ASR降至0.000，随后在第250步部分回升至0.035，第500步升至0.250；SFT的ASR在0.505-0.588之间。XSTest上R2D2的any-refusal从早期的1.000降至0.664和0.228。几何上，R2D2在第100步前保持后期层可拒绝载体，之后迁移到早期层载体，有效秩保持在1.23-1.27。因果干预表明低维但效用耦合的控制。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持拒绝载体重组（reorganization）而非简单漂移（drift-only）的解释，但证据限于一个骨干模型和固定来源攻击。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models must refuse harmful requests without collapsing into broad over-refusal, but the training-time mechanisms behind this tradeoff remain unclear. Prior work characterizes refusal directions and jailbreak robustness, yet does not explain how dynamic adversarial fine-tuning changes refusal carriers across training. We present a measurement-driven mechanism study, not a new defense, on one 7B backbone under supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and R2D2-style dynamic adversarial fine-tuning. Our protocol aligns fixed-source HarmBench, StrongREJECT, and XSTest with a five-anchor refusal-geometry suite and causal interventions. R2D2 drives fixed-source HarmBench ASR to 0.000 at steps 50 and 100, then partially reopens to 0.035 at step 250 and 0.250 at step 500; SFT remains less robust, with ASR between 0.505 and 0.588 at the same anchors. On XSTest, R2D2 any-refusal is 1.000 early, then falls to 0.664 and 0.228. Geometrically, R2D2 preserves a late-layer admissible carrier through step 100 before relocating to an early-layer carrier, while effective rank remains near 1.23--1.27. Causal interventions indicate low-dimensional but utility-coupled control. These results support a reorganization account rather than a drift-only account, with evidence limited to one backbone and fixed-source attacks.

</details>


### [103] [Cross-Subject Generalization for EEG Decoding: A Survey of Deep Learning Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27033)
*Taida Li,Yujun Yan,Fei Dou,Wenzhan Song,Xiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了针对跨被试脑电（EEG）解码中个体间差异导致的领域偏移问题，系统梳理了深度学习方法，包括特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四大类，并讨论了理论局限、被试身份价值和EEG基础模型等关键发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG解码中，被试间的高度变异性导致训练集与未见过测试集之间存在严重的领域偏移，阻碍了深度学习模型的泛化能力，亟需系统性方法来解决这一跨被试泛化挑战。

Method: 本文将跨被试EEG解码形式化为多源域问题，定义严格的主域独立评估协议，并对现有文献进行系统分类，归纳为特征对齐、对抗学习、特征解耦和对比学习四个方法论家族。

Result: 系统性地梳理并分类了当前跨被试EEG解码的深度学习方法，明确了各类方法的技术路线和适用场景。

Conclusion: 指出了三个关键方向：当前方法的理论局限性、被试身份的结构性价值，以及EEG基础模型的兴起，为未来实现鲁棒的、真实场景下的脑电解码提供了研究路线图。

Abstract: Deep learning for cross-subject EEG decoding is hindered by high inter-subject variability, which introduces a severe domain shift between training and unseen test subjects. This survey presents a comprehensive review of deep learning methodologies specifically engineered to address this cross-subject generalization challenge. To ground this analysis, we formalize the cross-subject setting as a multi-source domain problem and delineate the rigorous, subject-independent evaluation protocols required for valid assessment. Central to this survey is a systematic taxonomy of the current literature into discrete methodological families, including feature alignment, adversarial learning, feature disentanglement, and contrastive learning. We conclude by examining three critical elements for advancing robust, real-world decoding: the theoretical limitations of current methodologies, the structural value of subject identity, and the emergence of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [104] [Detecting Clinical Discrepancies in Health Coaching Agents: A Dual-Stream Memory and Reconciliation Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27045)
*Samuel L Pugh,Eric Yang,Alexander Muir Sutherland,Alessandra Breschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出双流记忆架构，分离患者自述与临床记录（FHIR），通过协调引擎检测记忆与临床数据间的差异。在675次纵向健康指导会话中，该架构检测出84.4%的临床差异，安全关键召回率为86.7%，并量化了13.6%的错误级联效应，其根源在于非结构化对话中记忆提取阶段丢失的临床细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体从单会话工具向管理纵向医疗旅程的持久系统过渡时，现有通用记忆系统倾向于用患者最新陈述覆盖旧事实以优化连贯性，这在临床数据中可能引发安全风险。患者自述虽及时但存在回忆偏差，电子健康记录（EHR）经医学验证却常已过时，两者皆为不完美的真相来源，亟需一种能协调二者的记忆架构。

Method: 提出双流记忆架构（Dual-Stream Memory Architecture），严格分离患者叙事流与结构化临床记录流（FHIR），并设计专门的协调引擎（Reconciliation Engine），对每条提取的记忆对照患者FHIR档案进行评估，按类型、严重程度及涉及的FHIR资源对差异进行分类。采用混合数据集（真实医患对话与合成FHIR临床场景）在26名患者675次纵向健康指导会话中评估。

Result: 在隔离测试中，协调引擎检测到84.4%的设计临床差异，安全关键召回率达86.7%。通过在同一数据上耦合提取与协调评估，直接量化了13.6%的错误级联效应，追溯发现退化源于非结构化对话中记忆提取阶段丢失的临床细节，而非下游分类错误。

Conclusion: 证实了将患者报告的记忆与临床记录进行验证在纵向健康智能体的安全部署中既可行又必要，为临床环境中LLM记忆系统的安全设计提供了方向。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) agents transition from single-session tools to persistent systems managing longitudinal healthcare journeys, their memory architectures face a critical challenge: reconciling two imperfect sources of truth. The patient's evolving self-report is current but prone to recall bias, while the Electronic Health Record (EHR) is medically validated but frequently stale. General-purpose agent memory systems optimize for coherence by overwriting older facts with the user's latest statement, a pattern that risks safety failures when applied to clinical data. We introduce a Dual-Stream Memory Architecture that strictly separates the patient narrative from the structured clinical record (FHIR), governed by a dedicated Reconciliation Engine that evaluates every extracted memory against the patient's FHIR profile and classifies discrepancies by type, severity, and the specific FHIR resources involved. We evaluate this architecture on 26 patients across 675 longitudinal wellness coaching sessions, using a hybrid dataset that interleaves real provider-patient transcripts with synthetic, FHIR-grounded clinical scenarios. In isolated testing, the engine detects 84.4% of designed clinical discrepancies with 86.7% safety-critical recall. By coupling extraction and reconciliation evaluation on the same data, we directly quantify a 13.6% error cascade, tracing the degradation to clinical details lost during memory extraction from unstructured conversation rather than to downstream classification errors. These findings establish that validating patient-reported memories against clinical records is both feasible and necessary for safe deployment of longitudinal health agents.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Rate Transfer in Normalized Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27077)
*Boris Shigida,Boris Hanin,Andrey Gromov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出νGPT参数化方案，解决了nGPT在学习率跨模型宽度、深度和token范围迁移上的问题，实现了超参数的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: nGPT虽然训练速度快且无需权重衰减和学习率预热，但其超参数无法随模型维度（宽度、深度）和token范围迁移学习率，这限制了其在不同规模模型间的超参数复用效率。

Method: 结合数值实验与alignment exponents理论，重新审视并修改μP超参数迁移方法，提出新的nGPT参数化方案νGPT。

Result: νGPT实现了学习率在宽度、深度和token范围上的跨模型迁移能力。

Conclusion: 通过νGPT参数化，成功修复了nGPT在学习率迁移方面的缺陷，使得超参数可以更高效地在不同规模模型间复用。

Abstract: The Normalized Transformer, or nGPT (arXiv:2410.01131) achieves impressive training speedups and does not require weight decay or learning rate warmup. However, despite having hyperparameters that explicitly scale with model size, we observe that nGPT does not exhibit learning rate transfer across model dimension and token horizon. To rectify this, we combine numerical experiments with a principled use of alignment exponents (arXiv:2407.05872) to revisit and modify the $μ$P approach to hyperparameter transfer (arXiv:2011.14522). The result is a novel nGPT parameterization we call $ν$GPT. Through extensive empirical validation, we find $ν$GPT exhibits learning rate transfer across width, depth, and token horizon.

</details>


### [106] [Co-Evolving Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27083)
*Naibin Gu,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Chuanyu Qin,Dingyu Yao,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出共进化策略蒸馏（CoPD），通过并行训练专家并在各专家RLVR训练过程中引入双向在线策略蒸馏，解决混合RLVR的跨能力分歧和流水线OPD的行为模式差距问题，实现文本、图像、视频推理能力的全合一整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR和OPD是后训练的标准范式，但混合RLVR存在跨能力分歧成本，而先训练专家再进行OPD的流水线则因师生行为模式差距大而无法充分吸收教师能力。需要一种统一分析并解决这两种范式能力损失问题的方法。

Method: 提出CoPD，并行训练多个专家，在每个专家进行RLVR训练过程中（而非训练完成后）引入OPD，使专家互为教师（双向OPD），实现共同进化，保持专家间一致的行为模式且维持互补知识。

Result: 实验验证CoPD实现了文本、图像、视频推理能力的全合一整合，显著优于混合RLVR和MOPD等强基线，甚至超越领域特定专家。

Conclusion: CoPD的模型并行训练模式可能启发新的训练扩展范式。

Abstract: RLVR and OPD have become standard paradigms for post-training. We provide a unified analysis of these two paradigms in consolidating multiple expert capabilities into a single model, identifying capability loss in different ways: mixed RLVR suffers from inter-capability divergence cost, while the pipeline of first training experts and then performing OPD, though avoiding divergence, fails to fully absorb teacher capabilities due to large behavioral pattern gaps between teacher and student. We propose Co-Evolving Policy Distillation (CoPD), which encourages parallel training of experts and introduces OPD during each expert's ongoing RLVR training rather than after complete expert training, with experts serving as mutual teachers (making OPD bidirectional) to co-evolve. This enables more consistent behavioral patterns among experts while maintaining sufficient complementary knowledge throughout. Experiments validate that CoPD achieves all-in-one integration of text, image, and video reasoning capabilities, significantly outperforming strong baselines such as mixed RLVR and MOPD, and even surpassing domain-specific experts. The model parallel training pattern offered by CoPD may inspire a novel training scaling paradigm.

</details>


### [107] [AutoSP: Unlocking Long-Context LLM Training Via Compiler-Based Sequence Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27089)
*Ahan Gupta,Zhihao Wang,Neel Dani,Masahiro Tanaka,Olatunji Ruwase,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoSP是首个自动化优化LLM长上下文训练的解决方案，通过自动序列并行和长上下文感知的激活检查点技术，在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上分别将训练上下文长度提升至2.7倍和2.5倍，且运行时性能损失可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM训练库（如ZeRO-3/FSDP、张量并行和流水线并行）主要针对大参数模型优化，缺乏针对长上下文训练的高层抽象，用户需要重写训练库来集成序列并行等复杂优化，这需要深厚的专业知识且降低开发效率。

Method: AutoSP采用自动化编译方法，对模型应用两组核心优化：1) 自动序列并行（sequence parallelism）; 2) 长上下文感知的激活检查点（activation-checkpointing）。整体实现自动化端到端优化，无需用户手动干预。

Result: 在NVIDIA和AMD硬件上，相比手工编写的高性能基线，训练上下文长度分别提升2.7倍和2.5倍，且运行时性能几乎无损失。

Conclusion: AutoSP首次实现了长上下文LLM训练的自动化优化，大幅降低了用户开发复杂度，同时在多种硬件平台上显著提升了可训练的最大上下文长度，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large-language-models (LLMs) demonstrate enormous utility in long-context tasks which require processing prompts that consist of tens to hundreds of thousands of tokens. However, existing LLM training libraries do not provide easy to use abstractions to optimize for long-context training, instead focusing on optimizations for models with large parameter counts through ZeRO-3/FSDP, Tensor and Pipeline parallelism. This forces users to rewrite LLM training libraries to incorporate compositions of various complex long-context optimizations, such as sequence-parallelism, to training pipelines; a process that requires in-depth expertise, reducing developer productivity. To tackle these challenges, we introduce AutoSP: the first automated solution to automatically optimize LLM training for longer-contexts. AutoSP compiles models and applies a targeted set of optimizations: automated sequence parallelism, and long-context aware activation-checkpointing, to drastically enhance LLM trainability at negligible cost to throughput. Our evaluation demonstrates AutoSP's capability on both NVIDIA and AMD hardware, increasing training contexts by upto 2.7$\times$ and 2.5$\times$ respectively over competitive hand-written baseline at negligible cost to runtime performance.

</details>


### [108] [Anomaly Detection in Soil Heavy Metal Contamination Using Unsupervised Learning for Environmental Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27102)
*Isaac Tettey Adjokatse,Samuel Senyo Koranteng,George Yamoah Afrifa,Theophilus Ansah-Narh,Marcellin Atemkeng,Joseph Bremang Tandoh,Kow Ahor Essel-Yorke,Richmond Opoku-Sarkodie,Rebecca Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究应用无监督机器学习框架（Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN及共识方法）检测加纳中部地区12个废物处置场地的重金属污染异常，识别出6个稳健异常样本（7.7%），均集中于S3场地，异常样本的平均危害指数（HI）比正常样本高约70-80%，且PCA重构误差与HI呈强正相关（r≈0.8），揭示了三种异常类型（极端Cu富集、异常低Ni、中等多金属共升高）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加纳快速城市化地区（尤其是非正规废物处置场）的重金属土壤污染持续威胁环境与公共健康，传统健康风险指标虽能评估风险水平，但缺乏对异常模式的精细识别能力，亟需客观、粒度的分析方法来定位高风险区域并指导环境管理优先序。

Method: 采集加纳中部地区12个废物场地及住宅对照点的78个土壤样本，分析As、Cd、Cr、Cu、Hg、Ni、Pb、Zn八种金属浓度及健康风险指标（HI和ILCR）；分别采用Isolation Forest、PCA重构误差、DBSCAN三种无监督方法检测异常，再通过共识方法整合稳健异常；通过相关性分析和异常类型分类进一步解析模式。

Result: Isolation Forest和PCA重构误差各识别12个异常样本（15.4%），DBSCAN未检测到密度孤立点；共识方法锁定6个稳健异常（7.7%），全部集中在S3场地；异常样本的HI均值比正常样本高约70-80%，且所有共识异常均超过HI=1阈值；PCA重构误差与HI呈强正相关（r≈0.8）；识别出三种异常类型：S3的极端Cu富集、S4/S5的异常低Ni、S9-S12的中等Pb-Zn多金属共升高。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习能够超越传统聚合指标，提供更客观、细粒度的异常检测结果，实现对高风险场地的精准优先级排序，为风险导向的环境管理提供有效决策支持。

Abstract: Soil contamination by heavy metals poses a persistent environmental and public health concern in rapidly urbanising regions of Ghana, particularly at unregulated waste disposal sites. This study applies an unsupervised machine learning framework to detect and characterise anomalous heavy metal contamination patterns in soils from twelve waste sites and residential controls in the Central Region, of Ghana. Concentrations of eight metals (As, Cd, Cr, Cu, Hg, Ni, Pb, Zn) were analysed alongside standard health risk indices, including the Hazard Index (HI) and Incremental Lifetime Cancer Risk (ILCR). Isolation Forest and PCA reconstruction error each identified $12$ anomalous samples ($15.4\%$ of $78$ samples), while DBSCAN detected no density-isolated noise points. A consensus approach isolated six robust anomalies ($7.7\%)$, all spatially concentrated at a single site (S3). Anomalies exhibited approximately $70$--$80\%$ higher mean HI values than normal samples, with all consensus anomalies exceeding the HI$=1$ threshold. PCA reconstruction error showed a strong positive association with HI ($r \approx 0.8$), indicating consistency between multivariate deviation and health risk. Three distinct anomaly types were identified: extreme Cu enrichment at S3, anomalously low Ni at S4/S5, and moderate multi-metal (Pb--Zn) co-elevation at S9--S12. The results demonstrate that unsupervised machine learning provides granular, objective insight beyond aggregate indices, enabling targeted site prioritisation and risk-informed environmental management.

</details>


### [109] [Better Models, Faster Training: Sigmoid Attention for single-cell Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27124)
*Vijay Sadashivaiah,Georgios Dasoulas,Judith Mueller,Soumya Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用Sigmoid注意力机制替代Softmax注意力机制训练生物基础模型，在六个单细胞数据集上实现了更好的细胞类型分离（提高25%）、更快的训练速度（提升10%）和更稳定的训练过程，并开源了高效GPU内核TritonSigmoid（在H100上达515 TFLOPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练稳定的生物基础模型需要重新思考注意力机制，Softmax注意力存在固有的不稳定性（梯度过大、密集耦合），影响模型训练效果和效率。

Method: 提出用Sigmoid注意力作为Softmax注意力的直接替换方案，分析了Sigmoid注意力的全局有界导数（≤0.25）和对角雅可比矩阵结构，从根本上缓解训练不稳定问题；并实现了高效GPU内核TritonSigmoid。

Result: 在六个单细胞数据集上，Sigmoid注意力比Softmax注意力：细胞类型分离提高25%，细胞类型凝聚指标更好，验证损失更低；训练速度提升10%；在1.6亿参数、8K序列长度的双向注意力模型压力测试中，Softmax梯度爆炸达四个数量级，Sigmoid保持稳定；TritonSigmoid在H100上达到515 TFLOPS，超越FlashAttention-2和FlashSigmoid。

Conclusion: Sigmoid注意力在理论和实验上均优于Softmax注意力，适合作为生物基础模型的注意力机制。

Abstract: Training stable biological foundation models requires rethinking attention mechanisms: we find that using sigmoid attention as a drop in replacement for softmax attention a) produces better learned representations: on six diverse single-cell datasets, sigmoid achieves 25% higher cell-type separation, better cell-type cohesion metrics, and lower validation loss, b) faster training, models with sigmoid attention train up to 10% faster than their softmax counterparts, and c) more stable training by eliminating inherent sources of instability in softmax attention. We establish that sigmoid attention has globally bounded derivatives ($\leq 0.25$) as opposed to softmax, and a diagonal Jacobian structure in contrast with softmax's dense coupling, which together help alleviate training instabilities. In stress tests on 160M-parameter bidirectional attention models trained without gradient clipping on 8K-token sequences, softmax diverges catastrophically, with gradients exploding by four orders of magnitude, while sigmoid remains stable. Finally, we implement and open-source TritonSigmoid, an efficient GPU kernel that achieves 515 TFLOPS on H100 GPUs, outperforming both FlashAttention-2 and FlashSigmoid, with native padding support, which is essential for biological sequences. Our results establish sigmoid attention as both theoretically grounded and empirically superior for biological foundation models. Code is available at https://github.com/MSDLLCpapers/triton-sigmoid

</details>


### [110] [How to Guide Your Flow: Few-Step Alignment via Flow Map Reward Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27147)
*Jerry Y. Huang,Justin Lin,Sheel Shah,Kartik Nair,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMRG框架，将引导问题重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，利用流映射实现无需训练、单轨迹的生成引导，在3步神经流评估内达到或超越基线水平，实现一个数量级以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导方法要么需要昂贵的多粒子、多步方案，要么依赖理解不足的近似，缺乏高效且理论基础扎实的引导方法。

Method: 将引导重新表述为确定性最优控制问题，推导出算法层次结构，利用流映射进行积分和引导，提出FMRG框架。

Result: 在文生图任务中，FMRG在逆问题、风格迁移、人类偏好和VLM奖励等任务上以仅3步NFE匹配或超越基线，相比先前最优方法实现至少一个数量级的加速。

Conclusion: FMRG是一种无需训练、单轨迹的高效引导方法，在极少的计算步骤下达到优异性能，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: In generative modeling, we often wish to produce samples that maximize a user-specified reward such as aesthetic quality or alignment with human preferences, a problem known as guidance. Despite their widespread use, existing guidance methods either require expensive multi-particle, many-step schemes or rely on poorly understood approximations. We reformulate guidance as a deterministic optimal control problem, yielding a hierarchy of algorithms that subsumes existing approaches at the coarsest level. We show that the flow map, an object of significant recent interest for its role in fast inference, arises naturally in the optimal solution. Based on this observation, we propose Flow Map Reward Guidance (FMRG): a training-free, single-trajectory framework that uses the flow map to both integrate and guide the flow. At text-to-image scale, FMRG matches or surpasses baselines across inverse problems, style transfer, human preferences, and VLM rewards with as few as 3 NFEs, giving at least an order-of-magnitude speedup in comparison to prior state of the art.

</details>


### [111] [Generalizing the Geometry of Model Merging Through Frechet Averages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27155)
*Marvin F. da Silva,Mohammed Adnan,Felix Dangel,Sageev Oore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模型合并应基于Fréchet平均框架，通过考虑对称性不变性在适当流形上进行参数合并，并针对LoRA适配器提出了实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数空间平均方法在架构对称性下表现脆弱，因为其几何结构未考虑对称性；且合并过程本身也需保持对称性不变。

Method: 将模型合并形式化为Fréchet平均（最小化流形上测地距离之和），通过选择合适的度量、流形和距离近似来实现对称感知合并；并在简化假设下证明该方法包含Fisher合并；针对LoRA的商流形几何提出了实用算法。

Result: Fréchet平均配合简化假设可恢复Fisher合并；提出的LoRA合并算法优于现有常见方法。

Conclusion: 模型合并的关键在于整体几何结构的设计（度量、流形和距离近似），而不仅是参数空间平均；对称性不变性对合并质量至关重要。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple models into one without additional training. Naïve parameter-space averaging can be fragile under architectural symmetries, as their geometry does not take them into account. In this work we show that not only the geometry, but also the averaging procedure itself, must be symmetry-invariant to achieve symmetry-aware merges. Consequently, we propose a general solution: merging as Fréchet averaging, i.e., selecting parameters that minimize a sum of geodesic distances on an appropriate manifold. In this view, the key design choice is the overall geometry, i.e., the choice of metric, manifold, and distance approximation, that determines what it means for two models to be "close". We show that Fréchet averaging, combined with simplifying assumptions, contains Fisher merging. Building on this, we examine the particular case of low-rank adapters (LoRA), whose symmetries induce a distinct geometry: that of a quotient manifold. We outline the limitations of current LoRA merging methods, propose a practical algorithm for this setting, and show how they compare with other commonly used approaches.

</details>


### [112] [Distributional Alignment Games for Answer-Level Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27166)
*Mehryar Mohri,Jon Schneider,Yifan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于博弈论的分布对齐框架，将答案级微调（ALFT）这个难以直接优化的边际化问题转化为可求解的投影问题，并证明其纳什均衡对应原问题的最优解。该框架统一了多样性与自改进方法，并兼容GRPO算法（如Coherence-GRPO），在数学推理任务上取得了显著的复杂度改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型微调方法通常基于推理轨迹优化，但答案级微调（直接根据最终答案的正确性或属性优化模型）由于需要对海量潜在推理路径进行边际化计算，导致优化在计算上不可行。

Method: 提出一个博弈论框架，将ALFT问题转化为一个双人分布对齐博弈：一方是策略（生成器），另一方是目标（辅助分布）。通过变分视角，将不可解的边际化问题转化为可解的投影问题。在该博弈中，纳什均衡等价于原始答案级优化问题的解。

Result: 该框架统一了现有的多样性增强与自改进（一致性）方法，并且与GRPO兼容，衍生出Coherence-GRPO等高效算法，在数学推理任务上取得了显著的复杂度增益。

Conclusion: 提出的博弈论框架为答案级微调提供了一种理论上严谨且计算可行的解决方案，统一了现有方法，并为高效推理优化算法（如Coherence-GRPO）提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We focus on the problem of \emph{Answer-Level Fine-Tuning} (ALFT), where the goal is to optimize a language model based on the correctness or properties of its final answers, rather than the specific reasoning traces used to produce them. Directly optimizing answer-level objectives is computationally intractable due to the need to marginalize over the vast space of latent reasoning paths. To overcome this, we propose a general game-theoretical framework that lifts the problem to a \emph{Distributional Alignment Game}. We formulate ALFT as a two-player game between a Policy (the generator) and a Target (an auxiliary distribution). We prove that the Nash Equilibrium of this game corresponds exactly to the solution of the original answer-level optimization problem. This variational perspective transforms the intractable marginalization problem into a tractable projection problem. We demonstrate that this framework unifies recent approaches to diversity and self-improvement (coherence) and provide efficient algorithms compatible with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), such as Coherence-GRPO, yielding significant complexity gains in mathematical reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Preserving Temporal Dynamics in Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27182)
*Ci Lin,Futong Li,Tet Yeap,Iluju Kiringa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MCMC的模型无关框架，通过对齐相邻时间点的经验转移统计量来修正条件生成模型在时序生成中累积的偏差，从而缓解分布偏移和时序漂移，提升多元时间序列合成数据的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GAN的时间序列生成方法仅关注边际分布匹配，忽略了原始多元时间序列中存在的时序动态性，导致生成序列出现分布偏移和时序漂移，降低了合成序列的保真度。

Method: 提出一个模型无关的MCMC框架，通过强制生成序列与原始数据相邻时间点之间的经验转移统计量保持一致，来修正条件生成模型在序贯生成过程中累积的偏差。

Result: 在Lorenz、Licor、ETTh和ILI四个数据集上，使用RCGAN、GCWGAN、TimeGAN、SigCWGAN和AECGAN五种生成模型进行实验，MCMC框架在自相关对齐、偏度误差、峰度误差、R²、判别分数和预测分数等指标上均取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 合成时间序列若要与原始数据保持一致性，必须显式保留转移规律，而非仅依赖对抗性分布匹配，这为改进时序数据生成建模提供了原则性方向。

Abstract: Time-series data augmentation plays a crucial role in regression-oriented forecasting tasks, where limited data restricts the performance of deep learning models. While Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) have shown promise in synthetic time-series generation, existing approaches primarily focus on matching marginal data distributions and often overlook the temporal dynamics that naturally exist in the original multivariate time series. When generating multivariate time series, this mismatch leads to distribution shift and temporal drift, thereby degrading the fidelity of the synthetic sequences.
  In this work, we propose a model-agnostic Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC)-based framework to mitigate distribution shift and preserve temporal dynamics in synthetic time series. We provide a theoretical analysis of how conditional generative models accumulate deviations under sequential generation and demonstrate that the MCMC algorithm can correct these discrepancies by enforcing consistency with empirical transition statistics between neighboring time points.
  Extensive experiments on the Lorenz, Licor, ETTh, and ILI datasets using RCGAN, GCWGAN, TimeGAN, SigCWGAN, and AECGAN demonstrate that the proposed MCMC framework consistently improves autocorrelation alignment, skewness error, kurtosis error, R$^2$, discriminative score, and predictive score. These results suggest that synthetic time series consistent with the original data require explicit preservation of transition laws rather than solely relying on adversarial distribution matching, thereby offering a principled direction for improving generative modeling of time-series data.

</details>


### [114] [AdaBFL: Multi-Layer Defensive Adaptive Aggregation for Bzantine-Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27434)
*Zehui Tang,Yuchen Liu,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AdaBFL的多层防御自适应聚合方法，用于拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习，该方法通过三层防御机制自适应调整防御算法权重，以应对多种投毒攻击，并在非凸非独立同分布数据上证明了收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习易受投毒攻击，现有拜占庭鲁棒方法难以对多种攻击提供均衡防御，或依赖服务器持有数据集。

Method: 提出AdaBFL方法，基于新颖的三层防御机制，能够自适应调整防御算法权重以对抗复杂攻击。

Result: 在多个数据集上的综合实验表明，AdaBFL优于对比算法。

Conclusion: AdaBFL能有效解决现有拜占庭鲁棒方法的缺陷，在多种攻击下实现均衡防御，且无需服务器持有数据集，并在非凸非独立同分布设置下具有收敛保证。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a popular distributed learning paradigm in machine learning, which enables multiple clients to collaboratively train models under the guidance of a server without exposing private client data. However, FL's decentralized nature makes it vulnerable to poisoning attacks, where malicious clients can submit corrupted models to manipulate the system. To counter such attacks, although various Byzantine-robust methods have been proposed, these methods struggle to provide balanced defense against multiple types of attacks or rely on possessing the dataset in the server. To deal with these drawbacks, thus, we propose an effective multi-layer defensive adaptive aggregation for Bzantine-robust federated learning (AdaBFL) based on a novel three-layer defensive mechanism, which can adaptively adjust the weights of defense algorithms to counter complex attacks. Moreover, we provide convergence properties of our AdaBFL method under the non-convex setting on non-iid data. Comprehensive experiments across multiple datasets validate the superiority of our AdaBFL over the comparable algorithms.

</details>


### [115] [Analytical Correction for Subsampling Bias in Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27239)
*Jiaru Zhang,Zeyun Deng,Juanwu Lu,Ziran Wang,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分析偏置校正（ABC）方法，通过闭式修正项将漂移模型中软max自归一化导致的O(1/n)偏置降低至O(1/n^2)，仅需两行额外代码即可在CIFAR-10上实现更快的训练和更低的FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移模型中的小批量质心估计存在由软max自归一化引起的O(1/n)偏置，该偏置会随着批量大小n减小而显著影响生成质量。直接纠偏需计算全分布期望，计算上不可行。

Method: 通过理论推导小批量质心偏置的主导项，提出分析偏置校正（ABC）——一种基于批内统计量的闭式修正项。该方法仅需两行额外代码，且在线性时间内完成计算。

Result: 理论证明ABC将偏置从O(1/n)降至O(1/n^2)，且不会一阶增加总方差，同时保持修正后质心的凸包包含性。玩具实验验证了偏置缩放阶数；CIFAR-10上ABC降低了FID并加速了训练，在小批量场景下收益最大。

Conclusion: ABC是一种简单高效、理论完备的偏置校正方法，能够在不增加计算开销的前提下显著提升漂移模型的生成质量，尤其适用于小批量训练场景。

Abstract: Drifting models are capable one-step generative models trained to follow a drifting field. The field combines attractive and repulsive softmax-weighted centroids over the data and current-generator distributions. In practice, only a minibatch of $n$ samples from each distribution is available, and each centroid is approximated by an empirical estimate. In this paper, we begin by showing that the minibatch centroid is in general a biased estimator of the target centroid, with a pointwise $O(1/n)$ bias arising from softmax self-normalization. Correcting this bias requires the expectation over the full distribution, which is intractable. We instead approximate the leading bias term from in-batch statistics and propose Analytical Bias Correction (ABC), a closed-form plug-in adjustment. We prove that ABC reduces the bias from $O(1/n)$ to $O(1/n^2)$, introduces no first-order increase in total variance, and preserves convex-hull containment of the corrected centroid. In practice, ABC requires only two additional lines of code and has negligible wall-time overhead under compiled execution. Toy experiments confirm the theoretical $O(1/n)$ and $O(1/n^2)$ scaling. On CIFAR-10, ABC reduces FID and trains faster, with the largest gains at small $n$, where the bias is most significant.

</details>


### [116] [Low Rank Adaptation for Adversarial Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27487)
*Han Liu,Shanghao Shi,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Chongjie Zhang,Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验证明，对抗扰动与LoRA类似，具有内在的低秩结构。基于此发现，本文提出将黑盒对抗攻击的搜索空间限制在低维子空间中，从而显著提升攻击的效率和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LoRA利用低秩矩阵高效更新LLM，而对抗样本生成与模型训练同属优化过程，因此自然提出疑问：对抗扰动是否也具备类似的低秩结构？探索这一性质以改进对抗攻击与防御。

Method: 两步法：1）利用参考模型和辅助数据将梯度投影到低维子空间；2）将黑盒攻击的扰动搜索限制在该低秩子空间内，从而减少查询需求并提升攻击效率。

Result: 在多种攻击方法、模型架构、数据集和威胁模型下，所提出的低秩对抗攻击相比传统方法在性能上取得了显著且一致的提升。

Conclusion: 对抗扰动具有内在的低秩结构，利用这一性质可以有效改进黑盒对抗攻击的效率与效果。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), which leverages the insight that model updates typically reside in a low-dimensional space, has significantly improved the training efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) by updating neural network layers using low-rank matrices. Since the generation of adversarial examples is an optimization process analogous to model training, this naturally raises the question: Do adversarial perturbations exhibit a similar low-rank structure?
  In this paper, we provide both theoretical analysis and extensive empirical investigation across various attack methods, model architectures, and datasets to show that adversarial perturbations indeed possess an inherently low-rank structure. This insight opens up new opportunities for improving both adversarial attacks and defenses. We mainly focus on leveraging this low-rank property to improve the efficiency and effectiveness of black-box adversarial attacks, which often suffer from excessive query requirements. Our method follows a two-step approach. First, we use a reference model and auxiliary data to guide the projection of gradients into a low-dimensional subspace. Next, we confine the perturbation search in black-box attacks to this low-rank subspace, significantly improving the efficiency and effectiveness of the adversarial attacks.
  We evaluated our approach across a range of attack methods, benchmark models, datasets, and threat models. The results demonstrate substantial and consistent improvements in the performance of our low-rank adversarial attacks compared to conventional methods.

</details>


### [117] [AutoREC: A software platform for developing reinforcement learning agents for equivalent circuit model generation from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27266)
*Ali Jaberi,Yonatan Kurniawan,Robert Black,Shayan Mousavi M.,Kabir Verma,Zoya Sadighi,Santiago Miret,Jason Hattrick-Simpers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoREC，一个基于强化学习的开源Python包，能够从电化学阻抗谱数据中自动生成等效电路模型，在合成数据集上成功率超过99.6%，并展现出对多种实验数据的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统等效电路模型识别依赖领域专家的手动试错，难以扩展至自主实验管线（如自驱动实验室），亟需自动化、数据驱动的ECM生成方法。

Method: 将ECM构建转化为马尔可夫决策过程中的序列决策问题，实现Double Deep Q-Network结合优先经验回放，并设计专用死循环缓解策略以高效探索电路生成的复杂动作空间。

Result: 在合成数据集上成功率超过99.6%，在电池、腐蚀、析氧反应和CO₂还原系统的实验EIS数据上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: AutoREC是自适应、数据驱动的ECM生成的有前景平台，有望集成至自动化电化学工作流中。

Abstract: This paper introduces AutoREC, an open-source Python package for developing reinforcement learning (RL) agents to automatically generate equivalent circuit models (ECMs) from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS) data. While ECMs are a standard framework for interpreting EIS data, traditional identification is typically based on manual trial-and-error, which requires domain experts and limits scalability, particularly in autonomous experimental pipelines such as self-driving laboratories. AutoREC addresses this challenge by formulating ECM construction as a sequential decision-making problem within a Markov Decision Process framework. It implements a Double Deep Q-Network with prioritized experience replay, along with a dedicated dead-loop mitigation strategy, to efficiently explore a complex action space for circuit generation. To demonstrate the capabilities of the platform, we trained an RL agent using AutoREC and evaluated its strengths and limitations across diverse datasets, while also discussing possible strategies to mitigate these limitations in future agent designs. The trained agent achieved a success rate exceeding $99.6\%$ on synthetic datasets and demonstrated strong generalization to unseen experimental EIS data from batteries, corrosion, oxygen evolution reaction, and CO$_2$ reduction systems. These results position AutoREC as a promising platform for adaptive and data-driven ECM generation, with potential for integration into automated electrochemical workflows.

</details>


### [118] [Predicting Covariate-Driven Spatial Deformation for Nonstationary Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27280)
*Minghao Gu,Weizhi Lin,Qiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于协变量驱动的空间形变方法，用于非平稳高斯过程的预测建模。通过将形变场建模为协变量的函数，并利用李代数理论截断高阶交互项，实现了在有限样本下的高效估计与预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的空间形变方法（静态扭曲）无法利用协变量信息预测新协变量条件下的非平稳高斯过程，限制了其在预测任务中的适用性。

Method: 将形变场建模为协变量的函数，通过速度场（李代数）生成微分同胚形变；证明在适度物理假设下可以截断多协变量的高阶交互项，从而得到简洁的形变函数形式；开发了高效的估计-推断算法。

Result: 通过仿真研究和两个案例（制造业、地质统计学）验证了方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法能够利用协变量驱动的形变实现非平稳高斯过程的外样本预测，兼具理论严谨性和计算可行性。

Abstract: Nonstationary Gaussian processes (GPs) are essential for modeling complex, locally heterogeneous spatial data. A common modeling approach is the spatial deformation method that warps the domain to recover isotropy. However, this static method does not account for changes in spatial correlation induced by covariates, limiting its ability to predict nonstationary GPs under new covariate conditions.
  To enable predictive modeling of the deformation method, we propose to model the spatial deformation as a function of covariates. The spaces of diffeomorphic deformations and Euclidean covariate vectors are connected by characterizing deformations as generated by velocity fields living in a Lie algebra. To overcome the estimation instability caused by high-order interactions between multiple covariates in a general Lie algebra, we prove that those interactions can be truncated with a moderate physical assumption. Based on the theoretical results, a concise functional form of deformations driven by multiple covariates can be established, and an efficient estimation-inference algorithm is developed for out-of-sample nonstationary GP prediction with limited covariate-deformation sample pairs. The effectiveness and generalizability of the method are demonstrated on a simulation study and two case studies, in the fields of manufacturing and geostatistics, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [PINN-Cast: Exploring the Role of Continuous-Depth NODE in Transformers and Physics Informed Loss as Soft Physical Constraints in Short-term Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27313)
*Hira Saleem,Flora Salim,Cormac Purcell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PINN-Cast，一种连续深度Transformer编码器，通过神经ODE替换离散残差更新、双分支注意力模块（含导数算子）和物理信息训练目标，在短期天气预报中优于离散Transformer和连续时间神经ODE基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值天气预报计算量大且流程复杂，而现有基于Transformer的数据驱动方法忽略了物理规律，其离散层更新机制不适合建模平滑的潜在动态。

Method: 1) 将Transformer编码器中的离散残差更新替换为基于神经ODE的连续深度更新，使用自适应数值积分求解；2) 设计双分支注意力模块，融合常规分块自注意力与对注意力logits施加导数算子的辅助分支；3) 提出物理信息训练目标，将物理一致性作为软约束纳入优化过程。

Result: 在短期天气预报任务上，PINN-Cast优于标准离散Transformer基线和现有连续时间神经ODE预测变体，验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 连续深度建模和物理信息约束能有效提升数据驱动天气预报的精度，为将物理先验融入深度学习气象预报提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Operational weather prediction has long relied on physics-based numerical weather prediction (NWP), whose accuracy comes at the cost of substantial compute and complex simulation workflows. Recent transformer-based forecasters offer efficient data-driven alternatives, however transformers are physics-agnostic models. Additionally, standard transformer encoders evolve representations through discrete layer updates that may be less suited to modeling smooth latent dynamics. In this work, we propose a continuous-depth transformer encoder for weather forecasting that integrates Neural Ordinary Differential Equation (Neural ODE) dynamics within each encoder block. Specifically, we replace discrete residual updates with ODE-based updates solved using adaptive numerical integration. We also introduce a two-branch attention module that combines conventional patch-wise self-attention with an auxiliary branch that applies a derivative operator to attention logits, providing an additional change-sensitive interaction signal. To further align forecasts with governing principles, we propose a customized physics-informed training objective that enforces physical consistency as a soft constraint. We evaluate the proposed method against a standard discrete transformer baseline and an existing continuous-time Neural ODE forecasting variant, demonstrating the importance of PINN-Cast in short term weather forecasting.

</details>


### [120] [AG-TAL: Anatomically-Guided Topology-Aware Loss for Multiclass Segmentation of the Circle of Willis Using Large-Scale Multi-Center Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27357)
*Jialu Liu,Yue Cui,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解剖引导的拓扑感知损失函数（AG-TAL），结合大规模多中心Circle of Willis数据集，通过半径感知Dice损失、断裂感知clDice损失和邻接感知共现损失，有效解决了3D多类血管分割中的类不平衡、拓扑断裂和边界混淆问题，在多个独立数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Circle of Willis（CoW）的多类精确分割对神经血管疾病管理至关重要，但现有深度学习方法存在血管断裂和跨类误分类问题，且当前拓扑损失函数在3D多类场景下计算成本过高。

Method: AG-TAL包含三个组件：(1) 半径感知Dice损失，解决小血管的类别不平衡问题；(2) 断裂感知clDice损失，利用组卷积高效保持局部连通性；(3) 邻接感知共现损失，利用解剖先验强制相邻动脉之间的清晰边界。同时引入大规模多中心CoW数据集进行训练。

Result: 5折交叉验证中，AG-TAL在所有CoW动脉上平均Dice得分为80.85%，小动脉提升1.05-3.09%。在六个独立数据集上，所有CoW动脉Dice得分在74.46%-81.17%之间，小动脉相比其他方法提升2.20%-9.98%。

Conclusion: AG-TAL在多类CoW动脉分割中表现出优越性，能很好地泛化到多个独立数据集。在阿尔茨海默病队列中的可靠性分析和临床应用验证了其鲁棒性及发现基于影像形态学生物标志物的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate multiclass segmentation of the Circle of Willis (CoW) is essential for neurovascular disease management but remains challenging due to complex vascular topology and variable morphology. Existing deep learning methods often suffer from vascular discontinuities and inter-class misclassification, while current topological loss functions incur prohibitive computational costs in 3D multiclass settings. To address these limitations, we propose an Anatomically-Guided Topology-Aware Loss (AG-TAL) and introduce a large-scale, multi-center CoW dataset with unified annotations to facilitate robust model training. AG-TAL specifically integrates a radius-aware Dice loss to address class imbalance in small vessels, a breakage-aware clDice loss that utilizes group convolutions to efficiently preserve local connectivity, and an adjacency-aware co-occurrence loss that leverages anatomical priors to enforce distinct boundaries between neighboring arteries. Evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation, AG-TAL achieved an average Dice score of 80.85% for all CoW arteries, with small arteries notably higher by 1.05-3.09% compared to state-of-the-art methods. Across six independent datasets, the performance of AG-TAL achieved Dice scores ranging from 74.46% to 81.17% for all CoW arteries, with improvements of 2.20% to 9.98% for small arteries compared to other methods.
